##########################################
######   POD COMBAT SCRIPTED GUIS   ######
##########################################

# Each scripted GUI handles a button click in the combat window.
# Scope: ROOT = player character (via GuiScope.SetRoot(GetPlayer.MakeScope).End)

###### MOVE BUTTONS ######

pod_combat_move_strike = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_strike_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_strike_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		pod_combat_can_use_miracle_of_protection_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_miracle_of_protection_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_move_deepening_shadows = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_deepening_shadows_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_deepening_shadows_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		pod_combat_use_psychic_burst_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}

pod_combat_move_valeren_ward = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_valeren_ward_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_valeren_ward_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	is_valid = {

		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_dark_invocation_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_dark_invocation_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_burebistas_throne_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_burebistas_throne_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.

				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_move_aura_of_decay = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_aura_of_decay_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_aura_of_decay_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_wood_ward_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_move_battle_howl = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_battle_howl_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_battle_howl_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		pod_combat_use_faerie_kin_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}

pod_combat_move_terrify = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_terrify_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_terrify_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}

pod_combat_move_speed_of_thought = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_speed_of_thought_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_speed_of_thought_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

pod_combat_move_voice_of_the_emperor = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_voice_of_the_emperor_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_voice_of_the_emperor_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		}
	}
}

pod_combat_move_angst_armor = {
	is_valid = {
		pod_combat_is_active_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_is_player_turn_trigger = yes
		pod_combat_can_use_angst_armor_trigger = yes
	}
	effect = {
		pod_combat_use_angst_armor_effect = yes
		# Flush workspace changes back to slot variables so the character
		# cards reflect the new HP / morale / soak / damage state immediately.
		pod_combat_save_workspace_effect = yes
		# If the current target was knocked out (or is no longer valid),
		# auto-retarget to the next living non-PC slot and reload it.
		# For single-pc, this also restores the player's previously
		# selected enemy/ally target since pc_target was never changed.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes } }
			pod_combat_restore_pc_target_effect = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { pod_combat_is_over_trigger = yes }
				pod_combat_current_ap_value < 1
			}
			pod_combat_end_player_turn_effect = yes
		}
	}
}
